568: export function buildScoreInstructions(config, lang, text = '', patterns = []) {
569:   // Skill/table surface: scoring-math core plus the markdown-table contract.
570:   // scoreText (src/scoring.js) embeds buildScoreMathCore directly and appends
571:   // its own strict-JSON contract instead.
572:   let inst = buildScoreMathCore(config, lang, text, patterns);
573: 
574:   inst += `\nOutput format (the Weight column must echo the values above verbatim):\n`;
575:   inst += `| Category | Weight | Detected | Raw Score | Weighted |\n`;
576:   inst += `|----------|--------|----------|-----------|----------|\n`;
577:   inst += `| **Overall** | | | | **XX.X (±10)** |\n`;
578: 
579:   return inst;
580: }

582: function buildPatternCounts(patterns = []) {
583:   return patterns
584:     .map((pack) => {
585:       const packName = pack.frontmatter?.pack || pack.file;
586:       const count = pack.frontmatter?.patterns;
587:       if (!packName || !Number.isFinite(Number(count))) return null;
588:       return `- ${packName}: ${Number(count)} patterns`;
589:     })
590:     .filter(Boolean);
591: }
592: 
593: function buildPatternCatalogDigest(patterns = []) {
594:   const lines = [];
595:   for (const pack of patterns) {
596:     const packName = pack.frontmatter?.pack || pack.file;
597:     if (!packName) continue;
598:     // List ALL pattern headings (one short name each). Truncating the digest
599:     // while the pattern-count section advertises the full frontmatter count
600:     // made the prompt claim denominators larger than the catalog it showed,
601:     // hiding patterns 7+ from prompt-guided detection (issue #397).
602:     const headings = Array.from(String(pack.body || '').matchAll(/^###\s+(?:\d+\.\s*)?(.+)$/gm))
603:       .map((match) => match[1].trim())
604:       .filter(Boolean);
605:     if (headings.length > 0) {
606:       lines.push(`- ${packName}: ${headings.join('; ')}`);
607:     } else {
608:       const summary = String(pack.body || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 160);
609:       if (summary) lines.push(`- ${packName}: ${summary}`);
610:     }
611:   }
612:   return lines;
613: }

615: // v3.11 Phase 3.2 helper: classify a text as "short" for scoring boost.
616: // Threshold: ≤200 non-whitespace chars OR ≤3 non-empty paragraphs.
617: /**
618:  * Classify whether text should use the short-text scoring boost.
619:  *
620:  * @param {string} text Text to inspect.
621:  * @returns {boolean} True when text is <=200 non-whitespace chars or <=3 paragraphs.
622:  * @example
623:  * const short = isShortText('A short note.');
624:  */
625: export function isShortText(text) {
626:   if (!text) return true;
627:   const stripped = text.replace(/\s+/g, '');
628:   if (stripped.length <= 200) return true;
629:   const paragraphs = text.split(/\n\s*\n/).filter((p) => p.trim().length > 0);
630:   return paragraphs.length <= 3;
631: }
632: 
633: // v3.11 minimal prompt — case-04 hypothesis test.
634: // Strips pattern definitions/examples and uses a casual instruction so the
635: // model's natural voice prior isn't overridden by analytical framing. Only
636: // invoked for rewrite mode; score/audit/diff stay on the strict
637: // path because they need precise pattern references.
638: function buildMinimalPrompt({ config, patterns, profile, persona = null, text, tone, documentSignals = null, jargon = 'keep', rewriteHeadings = false }) {
639:   const lang = config.language || 'ko';
640:   const activePatterns = patterns.filter((p) => !p.isScoreOnly);
641: 
642:   const watchWords = [];
643:   for (const pack of activePatterns) {
644:     const packName = pack.frontmatter?.pack || pack.file;
645:     const words = extractWatchWords(pack.body);
646:     if (words.length > 0) {
647:       watchWords.push(`- **${packName}**: ${words.join(', ')}`);
648:     }
649:   }
650: 
651:   const instruction = lang === 'ko'
652:     ? `이 글이 AI가 쓴 것 같아 보여서 사람이 쓴 것처럼 자연스럽게 다듬어줘. 아래 어휘들이 보이면 자연스러운 한국어로 풀어줘. 무리하게 의역하지 말고 의미·숫자·인과관계는 그대로 보존해. 분량도 원문과 비슷하게 유지해(대략 ±30% 이내) — 군더더기는 걷어내되 그 자리를 비슷한 무게의 자연스러운 표현으로 채우고, 요약문으로 줄여 버리지는 마.`
653:     : `This text reads like AI. Rewrite it so it sounds like a real person wrote it. If you spot any of the phrases below, swap them out for something natural. Don't over-paraphrase — keep the meaning, numbers, and causation intact. Keep the rewrite about the same length as the original (within roughly ±30%): cut the filler and hype, but replace them with natural phrasing of similar weight instead of compressing the text into a summary.`;
654: 
655:   // Document brief: without a global frame the model paraphrases block by
656:   // block in its own default voice and the result still reads AI. Same
657:   // contract as the strict prompt's Phase 0.
658:   const brief = lang === 'ko'
659:     ? `고치기 전에 글 전체를 먼저 읽고 속으로 파악해 둬 — 이 글이 무엇인지(랜딩페이지/블로그/공지/문서), 누가 누구에게 말하는지, 지배 어투가 무엇인지(해요체/합니다체/-다체), 반복되는 핵심 용어가 무엇인지. 재작성 내내 그 틀을 유지하고, 어투는 글의 지배 어투 하나로 통일해 — 어투가 문장마다 오락가락하는 것 자체가 AI 신호야. 핵심 용어는 일반 동의어로 바꾸지 말고 글이 쓰는 표현 그대로 재사용해. 파악한 내용은 출력하지 말고 본문에만 반영해.`
660:     : `Before editing, read the whole text and fix in your head: what this document is (landing page / blog post / notice / docs), who is speaking to whom, the dominant register and tone, and the recurring domain terms. Keep that frame throughout, unify every rewritten sentence to the document's dominant register — register drift between sentences is itself an AI tell — and reuse the document's own terms instead of generic synonyms. Never output this analysis; apply it to the body only.`;
661: 
662:   // Sentence-length rhythm: the single strongest deterministic AI signal is
663:   // uniform sentence length (low burstiness CV). Vocabulary swaps alone never
664:   // move it, so minimal mode must carry the instruction too — the strict
665:   // prompt's Phase 1 step 4 equivalent (inspection 2026-07).
666:   const rhythm = lang === 'ko'
667:     ? `문장 리듬도 반드시 다듬어. AI 글은 문장 길이가 자로 잰 듯 비슷한데, 균질한 문장 길이는 어휘를 아무리 바꿔도 사라지지 않는 가장 강한 AI 신호야. 문단마다 짧은 문장(5~8어절)과 긴 문장(20어절 이상)을 최소 하나씩 섞어서 길이 편차를 크게 벌려 — 긴 문장 하나를 "짧은 선언 + 긴 부연"으로 쪼개거나, 비슷한 길이 문장 두 개를 하나로 합치거나, 핵심 주장 뒤에 두어 어절짜리 토막 문장을 붙이는 식으로. 다 쓰고 나서 문장 길이를 훑어봤을 때 여전히 고만고만하면 그 문단은 다시 손봐.`
668:     : `Also fix the sentence rhythm. AI text keeps every sentence nearly the same length, and uniform sentence length is the strongest AI signal there is — no amount of vocabulary swapping removes it. In each paragraph mix at least one short sentence (5–8 words) with at least one long one (20+ words): split a long sentence into a blunt statement plus a longer follow-up, merge two same-length sentences, or tack a clipped two-word fragment after a key claim. When you finish, scan the sentence lengths — if they still look uniform, rework that paragraph.`;
669: 
670: 
671:   let prompt = `${instruction}\n\n${brief}\n\n${rhythm}\n\n`;
672:   const headingRule = buildHeadingPreservationRule(lang, rewriteHeadings);
673:   if (headingRule) prompt += `${headingRule}\n\n`;
674:   prompt += buildTransformDirective({ jargon, korean: lang === 'ko' });
675: 
676:   if (Array.isArray(documentSignals) && documentSignals.length > 0) {
677:     prompt += lang === 'ko' ? `## 문서 신호 (결정론 측정값)\n\n` : `## Document signals (measured)\n\n`;
678:     prompt += documentSignals.map((signal) => `- ${signal}`).join('\n');
679:     prompt += '\n\n';
680:   }
681:   prompt += buildKoreanAdvisoryRewriteGuidance(lang);
682:   const cjkGuard = buildCjkClauseRewriteGuard(lang);
683:   if (cjkGuard) {
684:     prompt += `${cjkGuard}\n`;
685:   }
686: 
687:   if (watchWords.length > 0) {
688:     prompt += lang === 'ko' ? `## AI 신호 어휘 (참고)\n\n` : `## AI signal words (reference)\n\n`;
689:     prompt += watchWords.join('\n');
690:     prompt += '\n\n';
691:   }
692: 
693:   // v3.11 Round 2 fix: profile body must reach the model in minimal mode too,
694:   // otherwise voice profiles like casual-conversation get ignored. Keep it
695:   // compact — just the profile body, no full pattern-overrides table.
696:   if (profile && profile.body) {
697:     prompt += lang === 'ko' ? `## 톤·프로필 가이드\n\n` : `## Tone & profile guide\n\n`;
698:     if (persona) {
699:       // Persona is the sole voice owner (v6.2): profile pattern policy applies,
700:       // all voice comes from the active persona (incl. the preserve default).
701:       const pn = config.profile || 'default';
702:       prompt += lang === 'ko'
703:         ? `- 프로필 "${pn}"의 패턴 정책은 적용되고, 어조는 아래 페르소나를 따른다.\n\n`
704:         : `- Profile "${pn}" pattern policy applies; voice defers to the persona below.\n\n`;
705:     } else {
706:       prompt += `${profile.body}\n\n`;
707:     }
708:   }
709: 
710:   if (tone && tone.tone_source) {
711:     prompt += lang === 'ko' ? `## 톤 메타\n` : `## Tone metadata\n`;
712:     prompt += `- tone: ${tone.tone === null ? 'null' : tone.tone}\n`;
713:     prompt += `- source: ${tone.tone_source}\n`;
714:     if (tone.tone_source === 'auto') {
715:       // Minimal mode previously emitted `tone: auto` without telling the model
716:       // to resolve it, so auto-tone quality diverged from the strict path (#527 H4).
717:       prompt += lang === 'ko'
718:         ? `- (auto: 본문에서 단일 톤을 추정해 적용하고, 아래 YAML 푸터의 tone/tone_evidence/tone_confidence를 그 값으로 채운다.)\n`
719:         : `- (auto: infer a single tone from the text, apply it, and fill the YAML footer's tone/tone_evidence/tone_confidence with the resolved values.)\n`;
720:     }
721:     prompt += `\n`;
722:   }
723: 
724:   if (persona) {
725:     prompt += formatPersonaDirective(persona, { lang, tone });
726:     prompt += '\n';
727:   }
728: 
729:   prompt += lang === 'ko' ? `## 출력 형식\n\n` : `## Output format\n\n`;
730:   prompt += `1. 다듬은 본문을 \`[BODY]\` ... \`[/BODY]\` 안에. 본문만, 머리말·메타·"최종 결과물" 같은 라벨 없이.\n`;
731:   prompt += `2. \`[SELF_AUDIT]\` ... \`[/SELF_AUDIT]\` 안에 짧게: 어떤 부분 손봤는지, 남은 AI 신호 있는지.\n`;
732:   prompt += `3. 톤 정보가 있으면 마지막에 YAML 푸터: \`---\\ntone: ...\\ntone_source: ...\\ntone_evidence: [...]\\ntone_confidence: ...\\n---\`\n\n`;
733: 
734:   prompt += lang === 'ko' ? `## 입력\n\n` : `## Input\n\n`;
735:   prompt += fenceInputText(text, { lang });
736:   prompt += lang === 'ko' ? `## 출력\n\n` : `## Output\n\n`;
737: 
738:   return prompt;
739: }
740: 
741: // Extract the comma-separated values that follow a "주의 어휘:" or "Watch words:"
742: // label in a pattern pack body. Used by buildMinimalPrompt to compress packs
743: // from full definitions+examples down to just the trigger vocab.
744: function extractWatchWords(body) {
745:   const re = /\*\*(?:주의 어휘|Watch words):\*\*\s*([^\n]+)/g;
746:   const out = [];
747:   let m;
748:   while ((m = re.exec(body)) !== null) {
749:     out.push(m[1].trim());
750:   }
751:   return out;
752: }

````javascript
 50: class SchemaError extends Error {
 51:   constructor(message, raw) {
 52:     super(message);
 53:     this.name = 'SchemaError';
 54:     this.raw = raw;
 55:   }
 56: }
 57: 
 58: function tryParseJson(s) {
 59:   try {
 60:     return { ok: true, value: JSON.parse(s) };
 61:   } catch {
 62:     return { ok: false, value: null };
 63:   }
 64: }
 65: 
 66: function isJsonObject(value) {
 67:   return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
 68: }
 69: 
 70: // Index of the `}` that closes the `{` at `open`, ignoring braces inside JSON
 71: // string literals (and their escapes). Returns -1 when unbalanced.
 72: function matchingBraceEnd(str, open) {
 73:   let depth = 0;
 74:   let inString = false;
 75:   let escaped = false;
 76:   for (let i = open; i < str.length; i++) {
 77:     const ch = str[i];
 78:     if (inString) {
 79:       if (escaped) escaped = false;
 80:       else if (ch === '\\') escaped = true;
 81:       else if (ch === '"') inString = false;
 82:       continue;
 83:     }
 84:     if (ch === '"') inString = true;
 85:     else if (ch === '{') depth++;
 86:     else if (ch === '}' && --depth === 0) return i;
 87:   }
 88:   return -1;
 89: }
 90: 
 91: function parseStrictJson(text) {
 92:   if (!text) throw new SchemaError('Empty response', text);
 93: 
 94:   let body = text;
 95:   const codeBlockMatch = text.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/);
 96:   if (codeBlockMatch) body = codeBlockMatch[1];
 97:   body = body.trim();
 98: 
 99:   // Common case: the body is exactly one JSON object.
100:   const whole = tryParseJson(body);
101:   if (whole.ok && isJsonObject(whole.value)) return whole.value;
102: 
103:   // Otherwise scan every balanced {...} span and keep the RICHEST object. A
104:   // naive indexOf('{')..lastIndexOf('}') slice breaks when prose carries stray
105:   // braces, e.g. "result for {A}: {\"overall\":20}" slices from `{A}` (#508 G2).
106:   // Returning the FIRST parseable object is also wrong when a chatty model
107:   // emits a stray/echoed object (or an empty `{}`) before the real score —
108:   // that nulls a valid score without a retry (#527 H8). And a lone unbalanced
109:   // '{' must skip, not abandon the scan, or a later valid object is missed
110:   // (#527 H9). Picking the object with the most keys favors the score object
111:   // (many keys) over a small echo while leaving the single-object case exact.
112:   let best = null;
113:   let bestKeys = -1;
114:   for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
115:     if (body[i] !== '{') continue;
116:     const end = matchingBraceEnd(body, i);
117:     if (end === -1) continue; // this '{' never balances; a later one might
118:     const candidate = tryParseJson(body.slice(i, end + 1));
119:     if (candidate.ok && isJsonObject(candidate.value)) {
120:       const keys = Object.keys(candidate.value).length;
121:       if (keys >= 1 && keys > bestKeys) {
122:         best = candidate.value;
123:         bestKeys = keys;
124:       }
125:     }
126:     i = end; // skip past this span
127:   }
128:   if (best !== null) return best;
129: 
130:   throw new SchemaError('No JSON object found', text);
131: }

133: // Call LLM and parse strict JSON. On schema failure, retry once at temperature 0.
134: async function callAndParseJson({
135:   prompt,
136:   apiKey,
137:   baseURL,
138:   model,
139:   temperature = 0.1,
140:   deadline,
141:   signal,
142:   timeout,
143:   callLLM = /** @type {Function} */ (defaultCallLLM),
144:   logger = createLogger(),
145:   now,
146:   sleep,
147:   // Opt-in OpenAI-compatible structured-output request field (e.g.
148:   // { type: 'json_object' }). Forwarded to callLLM on every attempt; the strict
149:   // JSON parse + temperature-0 retry below remains the fallback regardless.
150:   responseFormat,
151: }) {
152:   let lastError;
153:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
154:     const t = attempt === 0 ? temperature : 0;
155:     const result = await callLLM({
156:       prompt,
157:       apiKey,
158:       baseURL,
159:       model,
160:       temperature: t,
161:       deadline,
162:       signal,
163:       timeout,
164:       now,
165:       sleep,
166:       responseFormat,
167:     });
168:     try {
169:       return { parsed: parseStrictJson(result), raw: result };
170:     } catch (e) {
171:       lastError = e;
172:       if (attempt === 0) {
173:         logger.warn('score.json_parse_retry', {
174:           message: `[patina] score JSON parse failed (${e.message}); retrying at temperature 0`,
175:         });
176:       }
177:     }
178:   }
179:   throw lastError;
180: }

204:  */
205: export async function scoreText({
206:   text,
207:   config,
208:   patterns,
209:   apiKey,
210:   baseURL,
211:   model,
212:   deadline,
213:   signal,
214:   timeout,
215:   callLLM = defaultCallLLM,
216:   logger = createLogger(),
217:   now,
218:   sleep,
219:   // Opt-in structured-output request field; defaults off (undefined).
220:   responseFormat,
221: }) {
222:   const lang = config.language || 'ko';
223:   const deterministicScore = scoreDeterministicSignals({ text, config, logger });
224: 
225:   // buildScoreMathCore carries the shared scoring math (weights, severity
226:   // scale, denominators, catalog digest) but no output contract; the strict
227:   // JSON contract below is the ONLY contract in this prompt (issue #397).
228:   // The contract's example row and interpretation enum are derived from the
229:   // same sources as the math core (pack frontmatter counts × effective
230:   // severity points, SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS) so an override or band
231:   // relabel can never leave the contract contradicting the instructions.
232:   const prompt = `You are an AI-likeness scoring engine. Score the following text for AI-writing patterns.
233: 
234: ## Scoring Instructions
235: 
236: ${buildScoreMathCore(config, lang, text, patterns)}
237: 
238: ## Output Format (strict JSON)
239: 
240: Return ONLY a JSON object in this exact format (no markdown, no explanation):
241: 
242: {
243:   "categories": {
244:     ${buildContractExampleCategoryRow(patterns, config)},
245:     ...
246:   },
247:   "overall": 0.0,
248:   "interpretation": "${SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS.map((band) => band.label).join(' | ')}"
249: }
250: 
251: ## Text to Score
252: 
253: ${text}
254: `;
255: 
256:   try {
257:     const { parsed } = await callAndParseJson({
258:       prompt,
259:       apiKey,
260:       baseURL,
261:       model,
262:       deadline,
263:       signal,
264:       callLLM,
265:       timeout,
266:       logger,
267:       now,
268:       sleep,
269:       responseFormat,
270:     });
271:     return withShadowScore(parsed, { deterministicScore, config, logger });
272:   } catch (e) {
273:     rethrowIfAborted(e, signal);
274:     logger.warn('score.text_schema_failure', {
275:       message: `[patina] scoreText schema failure after retry: ${e.message}`,
276:     });
277:     return {
278:       overall: null,
279:       llmScore: { overall: null, interpretation: null, error: 'schema-failure' },
280:       deterministicScore,
281:       error: 'schema-failure',
282:       raw: e.raw,
283:     };
284:   }
285: }
286: 
287: // Build the example category row for the scoreText strict-JSON contract.
288: // Derived from the first loaded pack that declares a frontmatter pattern
289: // count, so the illustrated `max` always equals the pattern_count × high
290: // denominator the same prompt instructs the model to use — including under a
291: // `severity-points` override. Falls back to an illustrative 6-pattern
292: // "content" category when no pack metadata is available (mock/test paths);
293: // in that case the prompt carries no pattern-count claims to contradict.
294: function buildContractExampleCategoryRow(patterns, config) {
295:   const severityPoints = resolveSeverityPoints(config);
296:   const pack = (patterns || []).find((p) => Number.isFinite(Number(p?.frontmatter?.patterns)));
297:   const packName = String(pack?.frontmatter?.pack || 'content');
298:   // Category = pack name minus the language prefix (core/scoring.md §3).
299:   const category = packName.replace(/^[a-z]{2}-/, '');
300:   const patternCount = pack ? Number(pack.frontmatter.patterns) : 6;
301:   const max = patternCount * severityPoints.high;
302:   return `"${category}": {"detected": 0, "sum": 0, "max": ${max}, "score": 0.0, "weighted": 0.0}`;
303: }
304: 
305: /**
306:  * Compute deterministic stylometry/lexicon AI-likeness signals.
307:  *
308:  * @param {object} [options] Deterministic scoring options.
309:  * @param {string} [options.text] Text to analyze.
310:  * @param {object} [options.config={}] Effective config.
311:  * @param {string} [options.repoRoot] Repository root for analyzer resources.
312:  * @param {object} [options.logger] Optional logger for recoverable deterministic warnings.
313:  * @param {Function} [options.analyzer] Analyzer implementation.
314:  * @returns {object|null} Deterministic score payload, skipped payload, or null when disabled.
315:  * @example
316:  * const deterministic = scoreDeterministicSignals({ text: 'Draft', config });
317:  */
318: export function scoreDeterministicSignals({
319:   text,
320:   config = {},
321:   repoRoot = getRepoRoot(),
322:   analyzer = analyzeText,
323:   logger = createLogger(),
324: } = {}) {
325:   const options = deterministicScoringOptions(config);
326:   if (!options.enabled) return null;
327: 
328:   const lang = config.language || 'ko';
329:   const enabledLanguages = config.stylometry?.languages;
330:   if (Array.isArray(enabledLanguages) && !enabledLanguages.includes(lang)) {
331:     return {
332:       overall: null,
333:       interpretation: null,
334:       skipped: true,
335:       skipReason: 'language-disabled',
336:       paragraphCount: 0,
337:       hotParagraphs: 0,
338:       signalScore: 0,
339:       bands: emptyDeterministicBands(),
340:     };
341:   }
342: 
343:   try {
344:     const lexiconAllowed = isLexiconEnabledForLanguage(config, lang);
345:     let structuralModel = null;
346:     try {
347:       structuralModel = loadStructuralModel(config, { lang });
348:     } catch (err) {
349:       logger?.warn?.('score.structural_model_load_failure', {
350:         message: `[patina] structural model load failed; continuing without structural classifier: ${err?.message || err}`,
351:       });
352:     }
353:     const result = analyzer(String(text || ''), {
354:       lang,
355:       repoRoot,
356:       burstinessBands: config.stylometry?.burstiness?.bands,
357:       mattrBands: config.stylometry?.ttr?.bands,
358:       mattrWindow: config.stylometry?.ttr?.window,
359:       koDiagnosticsEnabled: config.stylometry?.ko_diagnostics?.enabled !== false,
360:       koDiagnosticBands: config.stylometry?.ko_diagnostics?.bands,
361:       lexiconDensityThreshold: config.lexicon?.density_threshold,
362:       structuralModel,
363:       ...(lexiconAllowed ? {} : { lexicon: { lang, path: null, strict: [], phrases: [] } }),
364:     });
365:     const paragraphs = Array.isArray(result?.paragraphs) ? result.paragraphs : [];
366:     const paragraphCount = paragraphs.length;
367:     const hotParagraphs = paragraphs.filter((p) => p.hot).length;
368:     const hotRatioOverall = paragraphCount > 0 ? roundScore((hotParagraphs / paragraphCount) * 100) : 0;
369:     // Model-output leakage (#332) is near-proof-grade and lives at the document
370:     // level, so it short-circuits the hot-ratio score into the 'heavily AI' band.
371:     const leaked = Boolean(result?.markupLeakage?.leaked);
372:     // Discourse tells (#334/#391) carry no document-level floor: the analyzer
373:     // attributes them to the paragraphs that carry the tell, so they reach the
374:     // score through the hot ratio like every other per-paragraph signal.
375:     const discourseTells = result?.discourseTells ?? null;
376:     const structuralClassifier = result?.structuralClassifier ?? { available: false, hot: null, score: null };
377:     const structuralFloor =
378:       structuralClassifier.hot === true && typeof structuralClassifier.score === 'number'
379:         ? Math.max(STRUCTURAL_CLASSIFIER_MIN_FLOOR, roundScore(structuralClassifier.score * 100))
380:         : 0;
381:     // All floors apply together. Previously leakage and the structural-classifier
382:     // floor were mutually exclusive, so a document that BOTH leaked and scored a
383:     // high structural floor was capped at the (lower) leakage floor — a near-proof
384:     // leakage token could LOWER the overall score. Take the max of every signal so
385:     // a floor can only ever raise the score (#527 H5).
386:     const overall = Math.max(
387:       hotRatioOverall,
388:       leaked ? LEAKAGE_SCORE_FLOOR : 0,
389:       structuralFloor,
390:     );
391:     const signalScore = roundScore(summarizeSignalStrength(paragraphs, {
392:       burstinessBands: config.stylometry?.burstiness?.bands,
393:       mattrBands: config.stylometry?.ttr?.bands,
394:       lexiconDensityThreshold: config.lexicon?.density_threshold,
395:     }));
396: 
397:     return {
398:       overall,
399:       interpretation: interpretScore(overall),
400:       skipped: Boolean(result?.skipped),
401:       skipReason: result?.skipReason ?? null,
402:       paragraphCount,
403:       hotParagraphs,
404:       signalScore,
405:       bands: {
406:         burstiness: countBands(paragraphs.map((p) => p.burstiness?.band)),
407:         mattr: countBands(paragraphs.map((p) => p.mattr?.band)),
408:         lexicon: {
409:           hot: paragraphs.filter((p) => p.lexicon?.hot).length,
410:           threshold: config.lexicon?.density_threshold ?? null,
411:         },
412:         koDiagnostics: {
413:           hot: paragraphs.filter((p) => p.koDiagnostics?.hot).length,
414:           thresholds: config.stylometry?.ko_diagnostics?.bands ?? null,
415:         },
416:         markupLeakage: {
417:           leaked,
418:           hits: Array.isArray(result?.markupLeakage?.hits) ? result.markupLeakage.hits.length : 0,
419:           floor: LEAKAGE_SCORE_FLOOR,
420:         },
421:         discourseTells: {
422:           hot: discourseTells?.hot ?? null,
423:           fakeCandor: discourseTells?.fakeCandor ?? null,
424:           thematicBreaks: discourseTells?.thematicBreaks ?? null,
425:         },
426:         structuralClassifier: {
427:           available: Boolean(structuralClassifier.available),
428:           hot: structuralClassifier.hot ?? null,
429:           score: structuralClassifier.score ?? null,
430:           floor: structuralClassifier.hot === true ? structuralFloor : 0,
431:         },
432:       },
433:     };
434:   } catch (err) {
435:     return {
436:       overall: null,
437:       interpretation: null,
438:       skipped: true,
439:       skipReason: 'deterministic-failure',
440:       paragraphCount: 0,
441:       hotParagraphs: 0,
442:       signalScore: 0,
443:       bands: emptyDeterministicBands(),
444:       error: err?.message || 'deterministic scoring failed',
445:     };
446:   }
447: }
448: 
449: /**
450:  * Merge an LLM score payload with deterministic shadow-score reconciliation.
451:  *
452:  * @param {object} parsed Parsed LLM scoring JSON.
453:  * @param {object} [options] Reconciliation options.
454:  * @param {object|null} [options.deterministicScore] Deterministic score payload.
455:  * @param {object} [options.config={}] Effective config.
456:  * @param {object} [options.logger] Logger for reconciliation warnings.
457:  * @returns {object} Score payload preserving llmScore and deterministicScore details.
458:  * @example
459:  * const score = withShadowScore({ overall: 20 }, { deterministicScore: { overall: 25 } });
460:  */
461: export function withShadowScore(parsed, { deterministicScore, config = {}, logger } = {}) {
462:   const llmOverall = toFiniteScore(parsed?.overall);
463:   const llmScore = {
464:     overall: llmOverall,
465:     interpretation: parsed?.interpretation ?? (llmOverall === null ? null : interpretScore(llmOverall)),
466:     categories: parsed?.categories ?? null,
467:   };
468:   const reconciliation = reconcileScoreOverall({
469:     llmOverall,
470:     deterministicScore,
471:     config,
472:     logger,
473:   });
474:   const overall = reconciliation.overall ?? llmOverall;
475:   return {
476:     ...parsed,
477:     overall,
478:     interpretation: overall === null
479:       ? parsed?.interpretation ?? null
480:       : interpretScore(overall),
481:     llmScore,
482:     deterministicScore,
483:     ...(reconciliation.scorePreference ? { scorePreference: reconciliation.scorePreference } : {}),
484:   };
485: }
486: 
487: /**
488:  * Reconcile LLM and deterministic overall scores according to config thresholds.
489:  *
490:  * @param {object} [options] Reconciliation inputs.
491:  * @param {number|null} [options.llmOverall] LLM overall score.
492:  * @param {object|null} [options.deterministicScore] Deterministic score payload.
493:  * @param {object} [options.config={}] Effective config.
494:  * @param {object} [options.logger] Logger for warnings.
495:  * @returns {{overall: number|null, scorePreference: (object|null)}} Reconciled score and preference source.
496:  * @example
497:  * const result = reconcileScoreOverall({ llmOverall: 20, deterministicScore: { overall: 60 } });
498:  */
499: export function reconcileScoreOverall({
500:   llmOverall,
501:   deterministicScore,
502:   config = {},
503:   logger,
504: } = {}) {
505:   const llm = toFiniteScore(llmOverall);
506:   const deterministic = toFiniteScore(deterministicScore?.overall);
507:   if (llm === null) return { overall: null, scorePreference: null };
508:   if (deterministic === null) return { overall: llm, scorePreference: null };
509:   if (deterministicScore?.skipped) return { overall: llm, scorePreference: null };
510: 
511:   const threshold = deterministicScoringOptions(config).divergenceThreshold;
512:   const delta = Math.abs(llm - deterministic);
513:   if (delta <= threshold) return { overall: llm, scorePreference: null };
514: 
515:   const overall = Math.max(llm, deterministic);
516:   const selected = overall === deterministic ? 'deterministic' : 'llm';
517:   const scorePreference = {
518:     reason: 'deterministic-divergence',
519:     selected,
520:     threshold,
521:     llmOverall: llm,
522:     deterministicOverall: deterministic,
523:     overall,
524:   };
525:   logger?.warn?.('score.deterministic_divergence', {
526:     message: `[patina] deterministic score diverged from LLM score (${llm} vs ${deterministic}); using pessimistic ${overall}`,
527:     llm_overall: llm,
528:     deterministic_overall: deterministic,
529:     selected,
530:   });
531:   return { overall, scorePreference };
532: }
533: 
534: /**
535:  * Score meaning preservation between original and rewritten text.
536:  *
537:  * @param {object} options MPS options.
538:  * @param {string} options.original Original text.
539:  * @param {string} options.rewritten Rewritten text.
540:  * @param {string} [options.apiKey] Provider API key.
541:  * @param {string} [options.baseURL] Provider base URL.
542:  * @param {string} [options.model] Model id.
543:  * @param {number} [options.deadline] Absolute epoch-millisecond deadline.
544:  * @param {AbortSignal} [options.signal] External cancellation signal.
545:  * @param {number} [options.timeout] Per-attempt backend timeout in milliseconds.
546:  * @param {Function} [options.callLLM] Injectable LLM implementation.
547:  * @param {object} [options.logger] patina logger.
548:  * @param {Function} [options.now] Clock returning epoch milliseconds.
549:  * @param {Function} [options.sleep] Sleep helper for tests.
550:  * @param {object} [options.responseFormat] Opt-in OpenAI-compatible structured-output request field forwarded to callLLM.
551:  * @returns {Promise<Object>} MPS result.
552:  * @throws {Error} When the operation is aborted.
553:  * @example
554:  * const mps = await scoreMPS({ original: 'A', rewritten: 'A', callLLM: async () => '{"mps":100,"anchors":[]}' });
555:  */
556: export async function scoreMPS({
557:   original,
558:   rewritten,
559:   apiKey,
560:   baseURL,
561:   model,
562:   deadline,
563:   signal,
564:   timeout,
565:   callLLM = defaultCallLLM,
566:   logger = createLogger(),
567:   now,
568:   sleep,
569:   // Opt-in structured-output request field; defaults off (undefined).
570:   responseFormat,
571: }) {
572:   // Anchor definition mirrors core/scoring.md §14-16 and SKILL.md step 4.5:
573:   // anchors are explicitly stated FACTUAL meaning units (claim/polarity/
574:   // causation/quantifier/negation), capped at 3 per paragraph. Stylistic
575:   // packaging (hype, intensifiers, boilerplate enthusiasm) is exactly what the
576:   // rewrite removes, so it must never be counted as an anchor — otherwise every
577:   // successful de-puffing scores as meaning loss (#579). The zero-anchor case
578:   // maps the spec's "MPS = N/A, gating exempt" to 100 because this JSON
579:   // contract has no N/A and consumers fail closed on null.
580:   const prompt = `You are a Meaning Preservation evaluator. Compare the ORIGINAL text with the REWRITTEN text.
581: 
582: Extract semantic anchors from the ORIGINAL text only, then verify whether each anchor survives in the REWRITTEN text.
583: 
584: Anchor types:
585: - claim: factual assertion or conclusion ("the system failed", "revenue grew 30%")
586: - polarity: positive/negative/neutral stance of a claim
587: - causation: cause-effect statement ("A caused B")
588: - quantifier: number, degree, or range ("p<0.05", "about 3x", "most")
589: - negation: negated statement ("does not", "never", "impossible")
590: 
591: Extraction rules:
592: - Extract only explicitly stated meaning. Never extract implications or subtext.
593: - Extract at most 3 anchors per paragraph of the original.
594: - Stylistic packaging is NOT an anchor. Intensifiers, marketing hype, and boilerplate enthusiasm ("cutting-edge", "we are thrilled to announce", "revolutionize your workflow", "unlock full potential", "take X to the next level", "comprehensive", "seamlessly") carry no factual content — removing or toning them down is the rewrite's job and must never be penalized as meaning loss.
595: - If the original contains no factual anchors at all, return "anchors": [], "pass_count": 0, "total_count": 0, "polarity_pass_count": 0, "polarity_total_count": 0, "mps": 100 (nothing meaning-bearing was at risk).
596: 
597: Verdict per anchor: PASS | SOFT_FAIL | HARD_FAIL
598: - PASS: the anchor's core assertion is unambiguously recoverable from the rewritten text; rephrasing is fine.
599: - SOFT_FAIL: present but weakened or made ambiguous.
600: - HARD_FAIL: deleted, contradicted, or polarity inverted.
601: - If the rewritten text fabricates new facts absent from the original (numbers, customers, outcomes, features), mark the closest related anchor HARD_FAIL. If facts were fabricated but no original anchor relates to them (including the zero-anchor case), add an anchor {"type": "claim", "content": "<the fabricated fact>", "verdict": "HARD_FAIL"} so fabrication always lowers mps — the mps-100 zero-anchor rule applies only when the rewritten text also adds no new facts.
602: 
603: Return ONLY a JSON object:
604: 
605: {
606:   "anchors": [
607:     {"type": "claim", "content": "...", "verdict": "PASS"}
608:   ],
609:   "pass_count": 0,
610:   "total_count": 0,
611:   "polarity_pass_count": 0,
612:   "polarity_total_count": 0,
613:   "mps": 0.0
614: }
615: 
616: MPS formula: (pass_rate × 0.6 + polarity_preserved × 0.4) × 100
617: If no polarity anchors: MPS = pass_rate × 100
618: 
619: ## Original
620: 
621: ${original}
622: 
623: ## Rewritten
624: 
625: ${rewritten}
626: `;
627: 
628:   try {
629:     const { parsed } = await callAndParseJson({
630:       prompt,
631:       apiKey,
632:       baseURL,
633:       model,
634:       deadline,
635:       signal,
636:       timeout,
637:       callLLM,
638:       logger,
639:       now,
640:       sleep,
641:       responseFormat,
642:     });
643:     return parsed;
644:   } catch (e) {
645:     rethrowIfAborted(e, signal);
646:     logger.warn('score.mps_schema_failure', {
647:       message: `[patina] scoreMPS schema failure after retry: ${redactErrorText(e.message)}`,
648:     });
649:     return { mps: null, error: 'schema-failure', raw: e.raw };
650:   }
651: }
652: 
653: /**
654:  * Convert a numeric AI-likeness score to a human-readable band.
655:  *
656:  * @param {number} score AI-likeness score from 0 to 100.
657:  * @returns {string} Interpretation band.
658:  * @example
659:  * const label = interpretScore(28); // mostly human
660:  */
661: export function interpretScore(score) {
662:   for (const band of SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS) {
663:     if (score <= band.max) return band.label;
664:   }
665:   // Above the last band max (e.g. unclamped >100): still the top band.
666:   return SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS[SCORE_INTERPRETATION_BANDS.length - 1].label;
667: }
668: 
669: // Length ratio is deterministic — bucket per core/scoring.md §10.4.
670: /**
671:  * Score rewritten length ratio on the 0-3 fidelity scale.
672:  *
673:  * @param {string} original Original text.
674:  * @param {string} rewritten Rewritten text.
675:  * @returns {number} Length-ratio points from 0 to 3.
676:  * @example
677:  * const points = lengthRatioPoints('abcd', 'abcde');
678:  */
679: export function lengthRatioPoints(original, rewritten) {
680:   if (!original || original.length === 0) return 3;
681:   const ratio = (rewritten.length / original.length) * 100;
682:   if (ratio >= 70 && ratio <= 130) return 3;
683:   if ((ratio >= 50 && ratio < 70) || (ratio > 130 && ratio <= 150)) return 2;
684:   if ((ratio >= 30 && ratio < 50) || (ratio > 150 && ratio <= 200)) return 1;
685:   return 0;
686: }
687: 
688: /**
689:  * Score fidelity between original and rewritten text using length plus LLM criteria.
690:  *
691:  * @param {object} options Fidelity options.
692:  * @param {string} options.original Original text.
693:  * @param {string} options.rewritten Rewritten text.
694:  * @param {string} [options.apiKey] Provider API key.
695:  * @param {string} [options.baseURL] Provider base URL.
696:  * @param {string} [options.model] Model id.
697:  * @param {number} [options.deadline] Absolute epoch-millisecond deadline.
698:  * @param {AbortSignal} [options.signal] External cancellation signal.
699:  * @param {number} [options.timeout] Per-attempt backend timeout in milliseconds.
700:  * @param {Function} [options.callLLM] Injectable LLM implementation.
701:  * @param {object} [options.logger] patina logger.
702:  * @param {Function} [options.now] Clock returning epoch milliseconds.
703:  * @param {Function} [options.sleep] Sleep helper for tests.
704:  * @param {object} [options.responseFormat] Opt-in OpenAI-compatible structured-output request field forwarded to callLLM.
705:  * @returns {Promise<Object>} Fidelity result.
706:  * @throws {Error} When the operation is aborted.
707:  * @example
708:  * const fidelity = await scoreFidelity({ original: 'A', rewritten: 'A', callLLM: async () => '{"criteria":{"meaning":3,"tone":3,"no_unintended_additions":3}}' });
709:  */
710: export async function scoreFidelity({
711:   original,
712:   rewritten,
713:   apiKey,
714:   baseURL,
715:   model,
716:   deadline,
717:   signal,
718:   timeout,
719:   callLLM = defaultCallLLM,
720:   logger = createLogger(),
721:   now,
722:   sleep,
723:   // Opt-in structured-output request field; defaults off (undefined).
724:   responseFormat,
725: }) {
726:   // Length is deterministic; only ask LLM for the three judgment criteria.
727:   const lengthPoints = lengthRatioPoints(original, rewritten);
728:   const lengthRatio = original ? Math.round((rewritten.length / original.length) * 100) : 100;
729: 
730:   const prompt = `You are a Fidelity evaluator. Compare ORIGINAL vs REWRITTEN text and score three criteria.
731: 
732: Each criterion: 0-3 points. High=3 (preserved), Medium=2 (minor drift), Low=1 (noticeable drift), Fail=0 (broken).
733: 
734: Criteria:
735: 1. claims_preserved — every factual claim in ORIGINAL appears (perhaps rephrased) in REWRITTEN.
736: 2. no_fabrication — REWRITTEN does not add claims/facts not present in ORIGINAL.
737: 3. tone_match — register/formality of REWRITTEN matches ORIGINAL.
738: 
739: Return ONLY this JSON, no markdown:
740: 
741: {
742:   "claims_preserved": 0,
743:   "no_fabrication": 0,
744:   "tone_match": 0,
745:   "rationale": "one sentence per criterion"
746: }
747: 
748: ## ORIGINAL
749: 
750: ${original}
751: 
752: ## REWRITTEN
753: 
754: ${rewritten}
755: `;
756: 
757:   let parsed = null;
758:   let schemaError = null;
759:   try {
760:     const result = await callAndParseJson({
761:       prompt,
762:       apiKey,
763:       baseURL,
764:       model,
765:       deadline,
766:       signal,
767:       callLLM,
768:       timeout,
769:       logger,
770:       now,
771:       sleep,
772:       responseFormat,
773:     });
774:     parsed = result.parsed;
775:   } catch (e) {
776:     rethrowIfAborted(e, signal);
777:     logger.warn('score.fidelity_schema_failure', {
778:       message: `[patina] scoreFidelity schema failure after retry: ${redactErrorText(e.message)}`,
779:     });
780:     schemaError = e;
781:   }
782: 
783:   const claims = clamp03(parsed?.claims_preserved);
784:   const noFab = clamp03(parsed?.no_fabrication);
785:   const tone = clamp03(parsed?.tone_match);
786:   const fidelity = ((claims + noFab + tone + lengthPoints) / 12) * 100;
787: 
788:   return {
789:     criteria: {
790:       claims_preserved: claims,
791:       no_fabrication: noFab,
792:       tone_match: tone,
793:       length_ratio: lengthPoints,
794:     },
795:     length_ratio_pct: lengthRatio,
796:     rationale: parsed?.rationale ?? null,
797:     fidelity: Math.round(fidelity * 10) / 10,
798:     ...(schemaError ? { error: 'schema-failure', raw: schemaError.raw } : {}),
799:   };
800: }
801: 
802: /**
803:  * Clamp and round a value into the inclusive 0-3 scoring range.
804:  *
805:  * @param {number|string} v Value to clamp.
806:  * @returns {number} Integer from 0 to 3.
807:  * @example
808:  * const value = clamp03(4.2); // 3
809:  */
810: export function clamp03(v) {
811:   const n = Number(v);
812:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
813:   if (n < 0) return 0;
814:   if (n > 3) return 3;
815:   return Math.round(n);
816: }
817: 
818: function rethrowIfAborted(err, signal) {
819:   if (signal?.aborted || err?.name === 'AbortError') throw err;
820: }
821: 
822: // Combined score per core/scoring.md §13: AI-likeness × ai_weight + (100 - fidelity) × fidelity_weight.
823: // Lower is better. Falls back to default weights if profile not configured.
824: /**
825:  * Combine AI-likeness, inverted fidelity, and optional deterministic score.
826:  *
827:  * @param {object} options Combined score inputs.
828:  * @param {number} options.aiLikeness AI-likeness score, lower is better.
829:  * @param {number} options.fidelity Fidelity score, higher is better.
830:  * @param {string} [options.profile] Profile name for configured weights.
831:  * @param {object} [options.config] Effective config.
832:  * @param {number|object|null} [options.deterministicScore] Optional deterministic score.
833:  * @returns {number} Combined score, lower is better.
834:  * @example
835:  * const score = combinedScore({ aiLikeness: 20, fidelity: 90, profile: 'default', config: {} });
836:  */
837: export function combinedScore({ aiLikeness, fidelity, profile, config, deterministicScore }) {
838:   const profileWeights = config?.ouroboros?.['combined-weights']?.[profile];
839:   const ai = profileWeights?.['ai-likeness'] ?? 0.6;
840:   const fid = profileWeights?.fidelity ?? 0.4;
841:   const deterministicWeight = deterministicScoringOptions(config).combinedWeight;
842:   const deterministic = toFiniteScore(deterministicScore?.overall ?? deterministicScore);
843:   const fidelityInverted = 100 - fidelity;
844:   if (deterministicWeight > 0 && deterministic !== null) {
845:     const totalWeight = ai + fid + deterministicWeight;
846:     return roundScore(
847:       (aiLikeness * ai + fidelityInverted * fid + deterministic * deterministicWeight) /
848:         totalWeight
849:     );
850:   }
851:   return roundScore(aiLikeness * ai + fidelityInverted * fid);
852: }
853: 
854: function isLexiconEnabledForLanguage(config = {}, lang) {
855:   if (config.lexicon?.enabled === false) return false;
856:   const enabledLanguages = config.lexicon?.languages;
857:   return !Array.isArray(enabledLanguages) || enabledLanguages.includes(lang);
858: }
859: 
860: function deterministicScoringOptions(config = {}) {
861:   const cfg = config.scoring?.deterministic || {};
862:   const enabled = cfg.enabled !== false;
863:   const divergenceThreshold = Math.max(0, positiveNumber(
864:     cfg['divergence-threshold'] ?? cfg.divergenceThreshold,
865:     DEFAULT_DETERMINISTIC_DIVERGENCE_THRESHOLD
866:   ));
867:   const combinedWeight = Math.max(0, positiveNumber(
868:     cfg['combined-weight'] ?? cfg.combinedWeight,
869:     0
870:   ));
871:   return { enabled, divergenceThreshold, combinedWeight };
872: }
873: 
874: function positiveNumber(value, fallback) {
875:   const n = Number(value);
876:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
877: }
878: 
879: function countBands(values) {
880:   const counts = { low: 0, mid: 0, high: 0, null: 0 };
881:   for (const value of values) {
882:     if (value === 'low' || value === 'mid' || value === 'high') counts[value]++;
883:     else counts.null++;
884:   }
885:   return counts;
886: }
887: 
888: function emptyDeterministicBands() {
889:   return {
890:     burstiness: { low: 0, mid: 0, high: 0, null: 0 },
891:     mattr: { low: 0, mid: 0, high: 0, null: 0 },
892:     lexicon: { hot: 0, threshold: null },
893:     koDiagnostics: { hot: 0, thresholds: null },
894:     markupLeakage: { leaked: false, hits: 0, floor: LEAKAGE_SCORE_FLOOR },
895:     discourseTells: { hot: null, fakeCandor: null, thematicBreaks: null },
896:     structuralClassifier: { available: false, hot: null, score: null, floor: 0 },
897:   };
898: }
899: 
900: function toFiniteScore(value) {
901:   if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return null;
902:   const n = Number(value);
903:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
904: }
905: 
906: function roundScore(value) {
907:   return Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
908: }
````

## File: tests/quality/benchmark.mjs
````javascript
  1: #!/usr/bin/env node
  2: // Quality benchmark for the deterministic stylometry layer.
  3: //
  4: // Iterates every fixture under tests/fixtures/suspect-zones/{lang}/{class}/*.md,
  5: // runs the in-tree analyzer (no LLM), and compares the predicted hot/cold
  6: // decision against the fixture's expected_hot label. Emits a per-language
  7: // confusion matrix + accuracy and writes the full per-fixture log to
  8: // tests/quality/results.json.
  9: //
 10: // Usage: node tests/quality/benchmark.mjs [--quiet]
 11: 
 12: import { readFileSync, readdirSync, writeFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
 13: import { resolve, dirname } from 'node:path';
 14: import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
 15: import yaml from 'js-yaml';
 16: 
 17: import { analyzeText } from '../../src/features/index.js';
 18: import { loadLexicon } from '../../src/features/lexicon.js';
 19: import { summarizeSignalStrength } from '../../src/features/signal-strength.js';
 20: import { summarizeRanking } from './ranking-metrics.mjs';
 21: import { summarizeSlices, lengthBucket } from './slice-metrics.mjs';
 22: import { resolveSliceFields } from './slice-metadata.mjs';
 23: 
 24: const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
 25: const REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '../..');
 26: const FIXTURES_ROOT = resolve(REPO_ROOT, 'tests/fixtures/suspect-zones');
 27: const RESULTS_PATH = resolve(__dirname, 'results.json');
 28: const EXPECTED_RANGES_PATH = resolve(FIXTURES_ROOT, 'expected-ranges.json');
 29: const FIXTURE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;
 30: 
 31: const FRONTMATTER_RE = /^---\n([\s\S]*?)\n---\s*\n([\s\S]*)$/;
 32: 
 33: function parseFixture(path) {
 34:   const raw = readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
 35:   const m = raw.match(FRONTMATTER_RE);
 36:   if (!m) throw new Error(`Missing frontmatter: ${path}`);
 37:   return { meta: yaml.load(m[1]), body: m[2].trim(), path };
 38: }
 39: 
 40: function listFixtures() {
 41:   const out = [];
 42:   for (const lang of readdirSync(FIXTURES_ROOT)) {
 43:     const langDir = resolve(FIXTURES_ROOT, lang);
 44:     if (!statSync(langDir).isDirectory()) continue;
 45:     for (const cls of readdirSync(langDir)) {
 46:       const clsDir = resolve(langDir, cls);
 47:       if (!statSync(clsDir).isDirectory()) continue;
 48:       for (const file of readdirSync(clsDir)) {
 49:         if (!file.endsWith('.md')) continue;
 50:         out.push(resolve(clsDir, file));
 51:       }
 52:     }
 53:   }
 54:   return out.sort();
 55: }
 56: 
 57: function loadExpectedRanges() {
 58:   const raw = readFileSync(EXPECTED_RANGES_PATH, 'utf8');
 59:   const ranges = JSON.parse(raw);
 60:   if (ranges?.schemaVersion !== 1 || !ranges?.metrics || typeof ranges.metrics !== 'object') {
 61:     throw new Error(`${EXPECTED_RANGES_PATH}: expected schemaVersion=1 and metrics object`);
 62:   }
 63:   return ranges.metrics;
 64: }
 65: 
 66: function emptyMetrics() {
 67:   return { tp: 0, fp: 0, fn: 0, tn: 0, total: 0 };
 68: }
 69: 
 70: function updateMetrics(m, predicted, expected) {
 71:   m.total++;
 72:   if (predicted && expected) m.tp++;
 73:   else if (predicted && !expected) m.fp++;
 74:   else if (!predicted && expected) m.fn++;
 75:   else m.tn++;
 76: }
 77: 
 78: function summarize(m) {
 79:   const accuracy = m.total ? (m.tp + m.tn) / m.total : 0;
 80:   const precision = m.tp + m.fp ? m.tp / (m.tp + m.fp) : 0;
 81:   const recall = m.tp + m.fn ? m.tp / (m.tp + m.fn) : 0;
 82:   const f1 = precision + recall ? (2 * precision * recall) / (precision + recall) : 0;
 83:   return {
 84:     ...m,
 85:     accuracy: round(accuracy),
 86:     precision: round(precision),
 87:     recall: round(recall),
 88:     f1: round(f1),
 89:     n: m.total,
 90:     ci_low: round(wilsonInterval(m.tp + m.tn, m.total).low),
 91:     ci_high: round(wilsonInterval(m.tp + m.tn, m.total).high),
 92:     confidence_method: 'Wilson score interval, 95%',
 93:   };
 94: }
 95: 
 96: function rankingRecords(fixtures) {
 97:   return fixtures.map((fixture) => ({
 98:     score: fixture.signal_score,
 99:     expected: fixture.expected_hot,
100:   }));
101: }
102: 
103: function summarizeRankingByLanguage(fixtures) {
104:   const byLanguage = {};
105:   for (const fixture of fixtures) {
106:     byLanguage[fixture.lang] ||= [];
107:     byLanguage[fixture.lang].push({
108:       score: fixture.signal_score,
109:       expected: fixture.expected_hot,
110:     });
111:   }
112:   return Object.fromEntries(
113:     Object.entries(byLanguage)
114:       .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
115:       .map(([lang, records]) => [lang, summarizeRanking(records)])
116:   );
117: }
118: 
119: function round(n, digits = 3) {
120:   return Math.round(n * 10 ** digits) / 10 ** digits;
121: }
122: 
123: function wilsonInterval(successes, n, z = 1.959963984540054) {
124:   if (!n) return { low: 0, high: 0 };
125:   const phat = successes / n;
126:   const denom = 1 + (z ** 2) / n;
127:   const center = (phat + (z ** 2) / (2 * n)) / denom;
128:   const margin = (z * Math.sqrt((phat * (1 - phat) + (z ** 2) / (4 * n)) / n)) / denom;
129:   return {
130:     low: Math.max(0, center - margin),
131:     high: Math.min(1, center + margin),
132:   };
133: }
134: 
135: function detectorHot(result) {
136:   return {
137:     burstiness: result.paragraphs.some((p) => p.burstiness?.band === 'low'),
138:     koDiagnostics: result.paragraphs.some((p) => p.koDiagnostics?.hot),
139:     mattr: result.paragraphs.some((p) => p.mattr?.band === 'low'),
140:     lexicon: result.paragraphs.some((p) => p.lexicon?.hot),
141:     endingMonotony: result.paragraphs.some((p) => p.endingMonotonyHot),
142:     candor: result.paragraphs.some((p) => p.candorHot),
143:     thematicBreak: result.paragraphs.some((p) => p.thematicBreakHot),
144:   };
145: }
146: 
147: function emptyDetectorMetrics() {
148:   return Object.fromEntries(
149:     Object.keys(detectorHot({ paragraphs: [] })).map((name) => [name, emptyMetrics()])
150:   );
151: }
152: 
153: function validateExpectedMetrics(path, expected = {}, observed = {}) {
154:   const failures = [];
155:   if (expected.cv_band && observed.cv_band !== expected.cv_band) {
156:     failures.push(`cv_band expected ${expected.cv_band}, got ${observed.cv_band}`);
157:   }
158:   if (expected.mattr_band && observed.mattr_band !== expected.mattr_band) {
159:     failures.push(`mattr_band expected ${expected.mattr_band}, got ${observed.mattr_band}`);
160:   }
161:   if (typeof expected.lexicon_density_min === 'number' && observed.lexicon_density < expected.lexicon_density_min) {
162:     failures.push(`lexicon_density expected >= ${expected.lexicon_density_min}, got ${observed.lexicon_density}`);
163:   }
164:   if (typeof expected.lexicon_density_max === 'number' && observed.lexicon_density > expected.lexicon_density_max) {
165:     failures.push(`lexicon_density expected <= ${expected.lexicon_density_max}, got ${observed.lexicon_density}`);
166:   }
167:   if (Array.isArray(expected.cv_range) && !inRange(observed.cv, expected.cv_range)) {
168:     failures.push(`cv expected ${formatRange(expected.cv_range)}, got ${observed.cv}`);
169:   }
170:   if (Array.isArray(expected.mattr_range) && !inRange(observed.mattr, expected.mattr_range)) {
171:     failures.push(`mattr expected ${formatRange(expected.mattr_range)}, got ${observed.mattr}`);
172:   }
173:   if (Array.isArray(expected.lexicon_density_range) && !inRange(observed.lexicon_density, expected.lexicon_density_range)) {
174:     failures.push(`lexicon_density expected ${formatRange(expected.lexicon_density_range)}, got ${observed.lexicon_density}`);
175:   }
176:   if (expected.detectors) {
177:     for (const [name, expectedHot] of Object.entries(expected.detectors)) {
178:       if (observed.detectors?.[name] !== expectedHot) {
179:         failures.push(`detector.${name} expected ${expectedHot}, got ${observed.detectors?.[name]}`);
180:       }
181:     }
182:   }
183:   if (typeof expected.predicted_hot === 'boolean' && observed.predicted_hot !== expected.predicted_hot) {
184:     failures.push(`predicted_hot expected ${expected.predicted_hot}, got ${observed.predicted_hot}`);
185:   }
186:   // Pins per-paragraph attribution (#391): a document-level OR could keep
187:   // predicted_hot true while leaving every paragraph cold, which this catches.
188:   if (typeof expected.hot_paragraphs === 'number' && observed.hot_paragraphs !== expected.hot_paragraphs) {
189:     failures.push(`hot_paragraphs expected ${expected.hot_paragraphs}, got ${observed.hot_paragraphs}`);
190:   }
191:   // A hot fixture must stay visible to the signal-score ranking leg.
192:   if (typeof expected.signal_score_min === 'number' && observed.signal_score < expected.signal_score_min) {
193:     failures.push(`signal_score expected >= ${expected.signal_score_min}, got ${observed.signal_score}`);
194:   }
195:   if (failures.length) {
196:     throw new Error(`${path}: expected_metrics regression failed: ${failures.join('; ')}`);
197:   }
198: }
199: 
200: function inRange(value, [min, max]) {
201:   return typeof value === 'number' && value >= min && value <= max;
202: }
203: 
204: function formatRange([min, max]) {
205:   return `[${min}, ${max}]`;
206: }
207: 
208: function main() {
209:   const quiet = process.argv.includes('--quiet');
210:   const fixtures = listFixtures();
211:   const expectedRanges = loadExpectedRanges();
212: 
213:   if (fixtures.length === 0) {
214:     console.error('No fixtures found under', FIXTURES_ROOT);
215:     process.exit(2);
216:   }
217: 
218:   const lexicons = {};
219:   const perLanguage = {};
220:   const byDetector = {};
221:   const fixtureLog = [];
222: 
223:   for (const path of fixtures) {
224:     const { meta, body } = parseFixture(path);
225:     const lang = meta.language;
226:     if (typeof meta.expected_hot !== 'boolean') {
227:       throw new Error(
228:         `${path}: \`expected_hot\` must be a literal boolean (got ${typeof meta.expected_hot}: ${JSON.stringify(meta.expected_hot)})`
229:       );
230:     }
231:     if (!lexicons[lang]) lexicons[lang] = loadLexicon(lang, REPO_ROOT);
232:     if (!perLanguage[lang]) perLanguage[lang] = emptyMetrics();
233:     if (!byDetector[lang]) byDetector[lang] = emptyDetectorMetrics();
234: 
235:     const result = analyzeText(body, {
236:       lang,
237:       lexicon: lexicons[lang],
238:     });
239:     const predicted = result.hot;
240:     const expected = meta.expected_hot;
241:     updateMetrics(perLanguage[lang], predicted, expected);
242:     const detectors = detectorHot(result);
243:     for (const [name, hot] of Object.entries(detectors)) {
244:       updateMetrics(byDetector[lang][name], hot, expected);
245:     }
246: 
247:     const p = result.paragraphs[0] || {};
248:     const observed = {
249:       cv: round(p.burstiness?.cv ?? 0),
250:       cv_band: p.burstiness?.band,
251:       mattr: round(p.mattr?.value ?? 0),
252:       mattr_band: p.mattr?.band,
253:       lexicon_density: round(p.lexicon?.density ?? 0),
254:       lexicon_hits: p.lexicon?.hits ?? [],
255:       ko_diagnostics_hot: Boolean(p.koDiagnostics?.hot),
256:       ko_diagnostics_reasons: p.koDiagnostics?.reasons ?? [],
257:       ko_diagnostics_strength: round(p.koDiagnostics?.strength ?? 0),
258:       signal_score: round(summarizeSignalStrength(result.paragraphs)),
259:       hot_paragraphs: result.paragraphs.filter((x) => x.hot).length,
260:     };
261:     const pinned = expectedRanges[meta.fixture_id];
262:     if (!pinned) {
263:       throw new Error(
264:         `${path}: missing benchmark regression range. Run node scripts/update-benchmark-ranges.mjs after reviewing the fixture.`
265:       );
266:     }
267:     observed.detectors = detectors;
268:     observed.predicted_hot = predicted;
269:     validateExpectedMetrics(path, pinned, observed);
270:     if (meta.expected_metrics) validateExpectedMetrics(path, meta.expected_metrics, observed);
271:     fixtureLog.push({
272:       fixture_id: meta.fixture_id,
273:       lang,
274:       class: meta.class,
275:       expected_hot: expected,
276:       predicted_hot: predicted,
277:       correct: predicted === expected,
278:       detectors,
279:       ...observed,
280:       expected_metrics: meta.expected_metrics ?? null,
281:       // Slice dimensions (B2, report-only). language/class/length_bucket are
282:       // always derivable; the rest default to `unspecified` until the corpus
283:       // carries that metadata.
284:       length_bucket: lengthBucket([...body].length),
285:       // generator/edited resolved via the tested B2 reconciliation mapper
286:       // (Wave 0.1): explicit B2-native fields win; model_family/edit_depth
287:       // aliases and class defaults fill the rest. register/domain pass through.
288:       ...resolveSliceFields(meta),
289:     });
290:   }
291: 
292:   const summary = {};
293:   let totalCorrect = 0;
294:   let totalCount = 0;
295:   for (const [lang, m] of Object.entries(perLanguage)) {
296:     summary[lang] = summarize(m);
297:     summary[lang].byDetector = Object.fromEntries(
298:       Object.entries(byDetector[lang]).map(([name, metrics]) => [name, summarize(metrics)])
299:     );
300:     totalCorrect += m.tp + m.tn;
301:     totalCount += m.total;
302:   }
303:   const overallAccuracy = totalCount ? totalCorrect / totalCount : 0;
304:   const overallCi = wilsonInterval(totalCorrect, totalCount);
305: 
306:   const results = {
307:     schemaVersion: 3,
308:     fixtureSchemaVersion: FIXTURE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
309:     nodeVersion: process.version,
310:     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
311:     fixtureCount: fixtureLog.length,
312:     overallAccuracy: round(overallAccuracy),
313:     overall: {
314:       accuracy: round(overallAccuracy),
315:       n: totalCount,
316:       ci_low: round(overallCi.low),
317:       ci_high: round(overallCi.high),
318:       confidence_method: 'Wilson score interval, 95%',
319:     },
320:     perLanguage: summary,
321:     ranking: {
322:       note: 'Signal-score ranking over the checked-in fixture corpus; diagnostic only, not a public generalization claim.',
323:       score: 'signal_score from the strongest deterministic paragraph trigger, averaged per fixture',
324:       overall: summarizeRanking(rankingRecords(fixtureLog)),
325:       perLanguage: summarizeRankingByLanguage(fixtureLog),
326:     },
327:     slices: summarizeSlices(
328:       fixtureLog.map((f) => ({
329:         language: f.lang,
330:         class: f.class,
331:         lengthBucket: f.length_bucket,
332:         domain: f.domain,
333:         register: f.register,
334:         generator: f.generator,
335:         edited: f.edited,
336:         predicted_hot: f.predicted_hot,
337:         expected_hot: f.expected_hot,
338:       }))
339:     ),
340:     fixtures: fixtureLog,
341:   };
342: 
343:   writeFileSync(RESULTS_PATH, JSON.stringify(results, null, 2) + '\n');
344: 
345:   const wrong = fixtureLog.filter((f) => !f.correct);
346: 
347:   if (!quiet) {
348:     console.log(`# Quality benchmark — ${fixtureLog.length} fixtures`);
349:     console.log(`Overall accuracy: ${(overallAccuracy * 100).toFixed(1)}%`);
350:     console.log(`Signal ROC-AUC: ${results.ranking.overall.roc_auc.toFixed(3)} · PR-AUC: ${results.ranking.overall.pr_auc.toFixed(3)} · best-F1 threshold: ${results.ranking.overall.bestF1.threshold}`);
351:     console.log();
352:     console.log('| lang | n | accuracy | precision | recall | f1 | TP | FP | FN | TN |');
353:     console.log('|------|---|----------|-----------|--------|----|----|----|----|----|');
354:     for (const [lang, s] of Object.entries(summary)) {
355:       console.log(
356:         `| ${lang} | ${s.total} | ${(s.accuracy * 100).toFixed(1)}% | ${(s.precision * 100).toFixed(1)}% | ${(s.recall * 100).toFixed(1)}% | ${s.f1.toFixed(2)} | ${s.tp} | ${s.fp} | ${s.fn} | ${s.tn} |`
357:       );
358:     }
359:     console.log();
360:     console.log('| lang | detector | n | accuracy | precision | recall | f1 | TP | FP | FN | TN |');
361:     console.log('|------|----------|---|----------|-----------|--------|----|----|----|----|----|');
362:     for (const [lang, s] of Object.entries(summary)) {
363:       for (const [detector, d] of Object.entries(s.byDetector)) {
364:         console.log(
365:           `| ${lang} | ${detector} | ${d.total} | ${(d.accuracy * 100).toFixed(1)}% | ${(d.precision * 100).toFixed(1)}% | ${(d.recall * 100).toFixed(1)}% | ${d.f1.toFixed(2)} | ${d.tp} | ${d.fp} | ${d.fn} | ${d.tn} |`
366:         );
367:       }
368:     }
369:     console.log();
370:     if (wrong.length > 0) {
371:       console.log(`Misclassified (${wrong.length}):`);
372:       for (const f of wrong) {
373:         console.log(
374:           `  ${f.fixture_id} (${f.class}) → predicted=${f.predicted_hot}, expected=${f.expected_hot} | cv=${f.cv} ${f.cv_band}, mattr=${f.mattr} ${f.mattr_band}, lex=${f.lexicon_density}/1000`
375:         );
376:       }
377:     } else {
378:       console.log('All fixtures classified correctly.');
379:     }
380:     console.log(`\nFull log: ${RESULTS_PATH}`);
381:   }
382: 
383:   // Non-zero exit on any misclassification so CI catches regressions even in --quiet mode.
384:   if (wrong.length > 0) process.exitCode = 1;
385: }
386: 
387: main();
````

## File: tests/quality/dogfood.mjs
````javascript
 1: #!/usr/bin/env node
 2: // Deterministic dogfood guard for public docs.
 3: //
 4: // This deliberately avoids live LLM calls in CI. It uses patina's in-tree
 5: // stylometry/lexicon analyzer and reports the percentage of prose paragraphs
 6: // that trip a hot signal. The threshold is a regression guard, not an
 7: // authorship verdict.
 8: 
 9: import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
10: import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path';
11: import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
12: 
13: import { scoreText } from '../../scripts/prose-score.mjs';
14: 
15: const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
16: const REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '../..');
17: const THRESHOLD = 30;
18: const TARGETS = [
19:   { file: 'README.md', lang: 'en' },
20:   { file: 'README_KR.md', lang: 'ko' },
21:   { file: 'README_ZH.md', lang: 'zh' },
22:   { file: 'README_JA.md', lang: 'ja' },
23:   { file: 'docs/FAQ.md', lang: 'en' },
24:   { file: 'docs/social/signs-of-ai-writing.md', lang: 'en' },
25:   { file: 'docs/social/signs-of-ai-writing_KR.md', lang: 'ko' },
26:   { file: 'SKILL.md', lang: 'ko' },
27: ];
28: 
29: function scoreFile({ file, lang }) {
30:   const raw = readFileSync(resolve(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8');
31:   return scoreText(raw, { file, lang, gate: THRESHOLD, repoRoot: REPO_ROOT });
32: }
33: 
34: const rows = TARGETS.map(scoreFile);
35: console.log('# Dogfood docs score');
36: console.log('| file | lang | paragraphs | hot | score | signal | pattern hits | threshold |');
37: console.log('|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|');
38: for (const r of rows) {
39:   console.log(`| ${r.file} | ${r.lang} | ${r.paragraphCount} | ${r.hotCount} | ${r.score.toFixed(1)} | ${r.signalScore.toFixed(1)} | ${r.patternHits} | ${THRESHOLD} |`);
40: }
41: 
42: const failures = rows.filter((r) => r.score > THRESHOLD);
43: if (failures.length) {
44:   console.error(`\nDogfood score exceeded ${THRESHOLD}: ${failures.map((f) => `${f.file}=${f.score.toFixed(1)}`).join(', ')}`);
45:   process.exitCode = 1;
46: }
````

````javascript
  3: /**
  4:  * Opt-in live rewrite quality runner.

  6:  * Default execution never calls a model: it scores fixture inputs and reports a

 12: import { dirname, extname, join, resolve } from 'node:path';

 15:
````
